Ledger deu_12_reeh_2026-09-20.md, coverage computed by script (197 sources), the ink facts computed from the Tanakh DB and the snapshot store (121 asserts — five fell on the first typed pass, forms not facts, retyped from the print), the engine's numeral parser measured on every verse (one number verse, 12:14 "in one of" [1]; one starred token, 12:17's tithe; no ordinal; no gap), no written/read pair in the chapter, every quotation cut by consonants (no miss). Seated as claims DV12-01..06: the header and the demolition: these are the statutes and the judgments which you shall keep to do in the land which the lord the god of your fathers has given you to possess, all the days that you live on the earth; destroy, you shall destroy all the places where the nations you are dispossessing served their gods, on the high mountains and on the hills and under every leafy tree; tear down their altars, break their pillars, burn their asherim in fire, cut down the images of their gods and destroy their name from that place; you shall not do so to the lord your god; the place chosen: but to the place which the lord your god will choose from all your tribes to put his name there — his dwelling you shall seek, and you shall come there; bring there your burnt offerings and your sacrifices, your tithes and the heave offering of your hand, your vows and your freewill offerings and the firstlings of your herd and your flock; eat there before the lord your god and rejoice in all you put your hand to, you and your households, wherein he has blessed you; you shall not do as all that we do here today, every man what is right in his eyes, for you have not yet come to the rest and the inheritance; when you cross the jordan and dwell in the land and he gives you rest from all your enemies round about and you dwell in safety, then the place which the lord your god will choose to make his name dwell there — there you shall bring all that i command you, your burnt offerings and sacrifices, your tithes and the heave offering of your hand and all the choice of your vows; and you shall rejoice before the lord your god, you and your sons and daughters, your menservants and maidservants, and the levite within your gates, for he has no portion nor inheritance with you; the burnt offerings only there: take heed to yourself lest you offer your burnt offerings in every place that you see; but in the place which the lord will choose in one of your tribes, there you shall offer your burnt offerings and there you shall do all that i command you; the profane slaughter, the blood and the gates: only, with all the desire of your soul you may slaughter and eat flesh according to the blessing of the lord your god in all your gates, the unclean and the clean may eat it, as the gazelle and as the hart; only the blood you shall not eat — on the earth you shall pour it like water; you may not eat within your gates the tithe of your grain, wine and oil, the firstlings of your herd and flock, your vows, your freewill offerings or the heave offering of your hand, but before the lord your god in the place he will choose — you and your son and daughter, your manservant and maidservant and the levite within your gates — and rejoice before the lord your god in all you put your hand to; take heed to yourself lest you forsake the levite all your days on your land; the border enlarged and the altar: when the lord your god enlarges your border as he has spoken to you and you say 'let me eat flesh', for your soul craves flesh — with all the desire of your soul you may eat flesh; when the place he will choose to put his name there is too far from you, slaughter of your herd and flock as i have commanded you and eat within your gates; as the gazelle and the hart are eaten so you shall eat it, the unclean and the clean together; only be steadfast not to eat the blood, for the blood is the life, and you shall not eat the life with the flesh — pour it on the earth like water, that it may go well with you and your children after you when you do what is right in the eyes of the lord; only your holy things and your vows you shall take up to the place he will choose, and offer your burnt offerings, the flesh and the blood, on his altar — the blood of your sacrifices poured on the altar and the flesh eaten; observe and hear all these words which i command you, that it may go well with you and your children after you forever, when you do the good and the right in the eyes of the lord your god; the nations cut off and the abomination: when the lord your god cuts off the nations whither you go to dispossess them, and you dispossess them and dwell in their land — take heed to yourself lest you be ensnared after them after they are destroyed from before you, and lest you inquire after their gods, saying: how did these nations serve their gods? and i will do so too; you shall not do so to the lord your god, for every abomination of the lord which he hates they did for their gods — even their sons and their daughters they burn in the fire to their gods. THE HEADER AND THE DEMOLITION: THESE ARE THE STATUTES AND THE JUDGMENTS WHICH YOU SHALL KEEP TO DO IN THE LAND WHICH THE LORD THE GOD OF YOUR FATHERS HAS GIVEN YOU TO POSSESS, ALL THE DAYS THAT YOU LIVE ON THE EARTH; DESTROY, YOU SHALL DESTROY ALL THE PLACES WHERE THE NATIONS YOU ARE DISPOSSESSING SERVED THEIR GODS, ON THE HIGH MOUNTAINS AND ON THE HILLS AND UNDER EVERY LEAFY TREE; TEAR DOWN THEIR ALTARS, BREAK THEIR PILLARS, BURN THEIR ASHERIM IN FIRE, CUT DOWN THE IMAGES OF THEIR GODS AND DESTROY THEIR NAME FROM THAT PLACE; YOU SHALL NOT DO SO TO THE LORD YOUR GOD. THE PLACE CHOSEN: BUT TO THE PLACE WHICH THE LORD YOUR GOD WILL CHOOSE FROM ALL YOUR TRIBES TO PUT HIS NAME THERE — HIS DWELLING YOU SHALL SEEK, AND YOU SHALL COME THERE; BRING THERE YOUR BURNT OFFERINGS AND YOUR SACRIFICES, YOUR TITHES AND THE HEAVE OFFERING OF YOUR HAND, YOUR VOWS AND YOUR FREEWILL OFFERINGS AND THE FIRSTLINGS OF YOUR HERD AND YOUR FLOCK; EAT THERE BEFORE THE LORD YOUR GOD AND REJOICE IN ALL YOU PUT YOUR HAND TO, YOU AND YOUR HOUSEHOLDS, WHEREIN HE HAS BLESSED YOU; YOU SHALL NOT DO AS ALL THAT WE DO HERE TODAY, EVERY MAN WHAT IS RIGHT IN HIS EYES, FOR YOU HAVE NOT YET COME TO THE REST AND THE INHERITANCE; WHEN YOU CROSS THE JORDAN AND DWELL IN THE LAND AND HE GIVES YOU REST FROM ALL YOUR ENEMIES ROUND ABOUT AND YOU DWELL IN SAFETY, THEN THE PLACE WHICH THE LORD YOUR GOD WILL CHOOSE TO MAKE HIS NAME DWELL THERE — THERE YOU SHALL BRING ALL THAT I COMMAND YOU, YOUR BURNT OFFERINGS AND SACRIFICES, YOUR TITHES AND THE HEAVE OFFERING OF YOUR HAND AND ALL THE CHOICE OF YOUR VOWS; AND YOU SHALL REJOICE BEFORE THE LORD YOUR GOD, YOU AND YOUR SONS AND DAUGHTERS, YOUR MENSERVANTS AND MAIDSERVANTS, AND THE LEVITE WITHIN YOUR GATES, FOR HE HAS NO PORTION NOR INHERITANCE WITH YOU [claim DV12-02]. = WITNESS-READ(the-border-enlarged-and-the-altar, as-he-has-spoken-let-from-eat-flesh-as-i-have-commanded-you-in-steadfast-the-blood-is-the-life-the-altar-observe-and-hear)
THE BORDER ENLARGED AND THE ALTAR: WHEN THE LORD YOUR GOD ENLARGES YOUR BORDER AS HE HAS SPOKEN TO YOU AND YOU SAY 'LET ME EAT FLESH', FOR YOUR SOUL CRAVES FLESH — WITH ALL THE DESIRE OF YOUR SOUL YOU MAY EAT FLESH; WHEN THE PLACE HE WILL CHOOSE TO PUT HIS NAME THERE IS TOO FAR FROM YOU, SLAUGHTER OF YOUR HERD AND FLOCK AS I HAVE COMMANDED YOU AND EAT WITHIN YOUR GATES; AS THE GAZELLE AND THE HART ARE EATEN SO YOU SHALL EAT IT, THE UNCLEAN AND THE CLEAN TOGETHER; ONLY BE STEADFAST NOT TO EAT THE BLOOD, FOR THE BLOOD IS THE LIFE, AND YOU SHALL NOT EAT THE LIFE WITH THE FLESH — POUR IT ON THE EARTH LIKE WATER, THAT IT MAY GO WELL WITH YOU AND YOUR CHILDREN AFTER YOU WHEN YOU DO WHAT IS RIGHT IN THE EYES OF THE LORD; ONLY YOUR HOLY THINGS AND YOUR VOWS YOU SHALL TAKE UP TO THE PLACE HE WILL CHOOSE, AND OFFER YOUR BURNT OFFERINGS, THE FLESH AND THE BLOOD, ON HIS ALTAR — THE BLOOD OF YOUR SACRIFICES POURED ON THE ALTAR AND THE FLESH EATEN; OBSERVE AND HEAR ALL THESE WORDS WHICH I COMMAND YOU, THAT IT MAY GO WELL WITH YOU AND YOUR CHILDREN AFTER YOU FOREVER, WHEN YOU DO THE GOOD AND THE RIGHT IN THE EYES OF THE LORD YOUR GOD [claim DV12-05]. THE BORDER ENLARGED AND THE ALTAR: WHEN THE LORD YOUR GOD ENLARGES YOUR BORDER AS HE HAS SPOKEN TO YOU AND YOU SAY 'LET ME EAT FLESH', FOR YOUR SOUL CRAVES FLESH — WITH ALL THE DESIRE OF YOUR SOUL YOU MAY EAT FLESH; WHEN THE PLACE HE WILL CHOOSE TO PUT HIS NAME THERE IS TOO FAR FROM YOU, SLAUGHTER OF YOUR HERD AND FLOCK AS I HAVE COMMANDED YOU AND EAT WITHIN YOUR GATES; AS THE GAZELLE AND THE HART ARE EATEN SO YOU SHALL EAT IT, THE UNCLEAN AND THE CLEAN TOGETHER; ONLY BE STEADFAST NOT TO EAT THE BLOOD, FOR THE BLOOD IS THE LIFE, AND YOU SHALL NOT EAT THE LIFE WITH THE FLESH — POUR IT ON THE EARTH LIKE WATER, THAT IT MAY GO WELL WITH YOU AND YOUR CHILDREN AFTER YOU WHEN YOU DO WHAT IS RIGHT IN THE EYES OF THE LORD; ONLY YOUR HOLY THINGS AND YOUR VOWS YOU SHALL TAKE UP TO THE PLACE HE WILL CHOOSE, AND OFFER YOUR BURNT OFFERINGS, THE FLESH AND THE BLOOD, ON HIS ALTAR — THE BLOOD OF YOUR SACRIFICES POURED ON THE ALTAR AND THE FLESH EATEN; OBSERVE AND HEAR ALL THESE WORDS WHICH I COMMAND YOU, THAT IT MAY GO WELL WITH YOU AND YOUR CHILDREN AFTER YOU FOREVER, WHEN YOU DO THE GOOD AND THE RIGHT IN THE EYES OF THE LORD YOUR GOD. 'When the LORD your God enlarges your border' one seat, plene (19:8 defective; Exodus 34:24 'and I will enlarge your border' the promise, the hiphil five in the Torah); 'AS HE HAS SPOKEN TO YOU' (12:20) the AS_WHEN form, four singular seats in the book (12:20, 15:6, 26:18, 29:12; 11:25 the plural), 'as He spoke' sixteen — the shelf disputes the callee (Genesis 15:19-20's three lands or Ezekiel 48's strips — 75:2, reread whole from two Genesis sittings): a RUN_CITATION pointer for the compile's census with two teachers; 'let me eat flesh' one seat, 'for your soul craves to eat flesh' one, 'flesh' seven in the chapter (four in 12:20); 'when the place is too far from you' 12:21 and 14:24 (twelve tokens shared); 'AS I HAVE COMMANDED YOU' (12:21) — THE RECEIPT WITHOUT THE NAME, two seats in the Bible (12:21 and Exodus 23:15's unleavened bread), 'as I commanded' six and 'as … commanded' three in the book, the register's finder BLIND to it (measured — its two forms carry the Name; no receipt found in chapter 12; the header at 12:1 already on the register index as a declared EMPTY seat): the shelf makes it THE ORAL LAW'S SEAT — the rite of slaughter taught for the offerings (75:6 'just as holy things by slaughter, so common'), the gullet and the windpipe commanded to Moses and written nowhere (75:15 — Mishnah Chullin 2:1; Chullin 28a at the compile's docket); the finder's third form owed since 4b and 6b, 12:21 its fourth seat; 'of your herd and of your flock' the partitive (75:5 — Chullin 84a-b's civility at the docket); 'but' (12:22) the chapter's one, 'the gazelle and the hart' with the article one seat — THE COMPARISON THAT TEACHES AND IS TAUGHT (75:15 reads it as teaching the wild animal's slaughter and taught the bird's by the scribes), 'the unclean and the clean together' one dish, not the heave offering (75:11); 'BE STEADFAST' (12:23) the word said to Joshua (1:38, 31:7, 31:23) said to the eater — Israel steeped in blood before Sinai (76:1), the lightest commandment the measure of all (76:2, 76:9, 286:16 — Mishnah Makkot 3:15); 'THE BLOOD IS THE LIFE' — Leviticus 17:11's clause turned (three tokens shared), Genesis 9:4's none; 'the life with the flesh' the limb from the living (76:5), flesh in milk from 'you shall not eat it' (76:7-8 — Mishnah Chullin 8:1) by refuted a fortiori arguments; 'that it may go well with you and your children after you' 12:25 and 12:28 alone, 'what is right in the eyes of the LORD' twenty-one seats (21:9 the kin); 'your holy things' (12:26) the Bible's one seat — from abroad (77:1), the substitute (77:6, 78:9 — Mishnah Temurah 3:5), 'take up and go' one seat; 'SHALL BE POURED' the sin offering's verb (Leviticus 4:7, 18, 25, 30, 34) said of the sacrifices' blood at 12:27 — no blood no flesh (78:1-2 — Tosefta Zevachim 4:1), the one application (78:7-8 — Zevachim 37a), the bones and the sinews (78:4-5 — Mishnah Zevachim 9:5-6); 'OBSERVE AND HEAR' (12:28) the imperative and the perfect one seat — the ladder of learning (79:1 — Avot 2:1), 'forever' the book's four, 'THE GOOD AND THE RIGHT' 6:18's pair and Kings' measure of a king — the good in Heaven's eyes, the right in men's (79:5 — Tosefta Sheqalim 2:1). Onkelos: 'as the FLESH of the gazelle is eaten' supplied at 12:22, THE TITHE SUPPLIED at 12:26 ('the tithe of your holy things' — the Aramaic's five tithe rows, 12:26 the one where the Hebrew has none), 'keep and RECEIVE' for 'observe and hear' at 12:28, 'what is fit before the LORD' at 12:25. 		—Onkelos Deut 12:20, Onkelos Deut 12:21, Onkelos Deut 12:22, Onkelos Deut 12:23, Onkelos Deut 12:24, Onkelos Deut 12:25, Onkelos Deut 12:26, Onkelos Deut 12:27, Onkelos Deut 12:28, Sifrei Devarim 75:1, Sifrei Devarim 75:2, Sifrei Devarim 75:3, Sifrei Devarim 75:4, Sifrei Devarim 75:5, Sifrei Devarim 75:6, Sifrei Devarim 75:7, Sifrei Devarim 75:8, Sifrei Devarim 75:9, Sifrei Devarim 75:10, Sifrei Devarim 75:11, Sifrei Devarim 75:12, Sifrei Devarim 75:13, Sifrei Devarim 75:14, Sifrei Devarim 75:15, Sifrei Devarim 76:1, Sifrei Devarim 76:2, Sifrei Devarim 76:3, Sifrei Devarim 76:4, Sifrei Devarim 76:5, Sifrei Devarim 76:6, Sifrei Devarim 76:7, Sifrei Devarim 76:8, Sifrei Devarim 76:9, Sifrei Devarim 77:1, Sifrei Devarim 77:2, Sifrei Devarim 77:3, Sifrei Devarim 77:4, Sifrei Devarim 77:5, Sifrei Devarim 77:6, Sifrei Devarim 77:7, Sifrei Devarim 77:8, Sifrei Devarim 77:9, Sifrei Devarim 78:1, Sifrei Devarim 78:2, Sifrei Devarim 78:3, Sifrei Devarim 78:4, Sifrei Devarim 78:5, Sifrei Devarim 78:6, Sifrei Devarim 78:7, Sifrei Devarim 78:8, Sifrei Devarim 78:9, Sifrei Devarim 78:10, Sifrei Devarim 79:1, Sifrei Devarim 79:2, Sifrei Devarim 79:3, Sifrei Devarim 79:4, Sifrei Devarim 79:5, Sifrei Devarim 286:16witnessed

WITNESS_READWITNESS-READ(the_nations_cut_off_and_the_abomination, when_the_lord_cuts_off_the_nations_lest_you_be_ensnared_how_did_they_serve_not_so_to_the_lord_the_children_burned)
= WITNESS-READ(the-nations-cut-off-and-the-abomination, when-the-lord-cuts-off-the-nations-lest-you-in-ensnared-how-did-they-serve-not-so-to-the-lord-the-children-burned)
THE NATIONS CUT OFF AND THE ABOMINATION: WHEN THE LORD YOUR GOD CUTS OFF THE NATIONS WHITHER YOU GO TO DISPOSSESS THEM, AND YOU DISPOSSESS THEM AND DWELL IN THEIR LAND — TAKE HEED TO YOURSELF LEST YOU BE ENSNARED AFTER THEM AFTER THEY ARE DESTROYED FROM BEFORE YOU, AND LEST YOU INQUIRE AFTER THEIR GODS, SAYING: HOW DID THESE NATIONS SERVE THEIR GODS? AND I WILL DO SO TOO; YOU SHALL NOT DO SO TO THE LORD YOUR GOD, FOR EVERY ABOMINATION OF THE LORD WHICH HE HATES THEY DID FOR THEIR GODS — EVEN THEIR SONS AND THEIR DAUGHTERS THEY BURN IN THE FIRE TO THEIR GODS [claim DV12-06]. THE NATIONS CUT OFF AND THE ABOMINATION: WHEN THE LORD YOUR GOD CUTS OFF THE NATIONS WHITHER YOU GO TO DISPOSSESS THEM, AND YOU DISPOSSESS THEM AND DWELL IN THEIR LAND — TAKE HEED TO YOURSELF LEST YOU BE ENSNARED AFTER THEM AFTER THEY ARE DESTROYED FROM BEFORE YOU, AND LEST YOU INQUIRE AFTER THEIR GODS, SAYING: HOW DID THESE NATIONS SERVE THEIR GODS? AND I WILL DO SO TOO; YOU SHALL NOT DO SO TO THE LORD YOUR GOD, FOR EVERY ABOMINATION OF THE LORD WHICH HE HATES THEY DID FOR THEIR GODS — EVEN THEIR SONS AND THEIR DAUGHTERS THEY BURN IN THE FIRE TO THEIR GODS. 'When the LORD your God cuts off the nations' 12:29 and 19:1 (eight tokens; 7:1 eight), 'whither you go to dispossess them' one seat, 'and you dispossess them and dwell in their land' one — wherever you wish to build, build (80:3; 179:2 from 19:1's side, the English's '(Dt.13:29)' a wrong chapter and the Hebrew's citation right, asserted); the sages weeping at the border (80:4-5, reread whole from chapter 11); 'LEST YOU BE ENSNARED AFTER THEM' one seat — the root five in the Bible and the niphal 12:30 the one, 7:25's 'lest you be snared' ANOTHER ROOT, the Torah's one niphal of each; 'after they are destroyed from before you' one seat; 'lest you inquire after their gods' one — 12:5's verb of seeking; 'how' six in the Torah (1:12, 7:17, 12:30, 18:21, 32:30; Genesis 3:9); 'and I will do so, I too' one seat — the chapter's one 'saying', the seeker's voice, no divine frame anywhere in the chapter; the third 'take heed' a prohibition (81:1); THE ABOMINATION'S SCOPE — 'so' for the service and for the thing served (81:5 — the wrong altar's liability keyed to the Temple's rite; 12:31 the singular pair of 12:4's plural), the parents included from 'even' (81:6 — R. Akiva's witness); 'every abomination of the LORD which He hates' one seat, 'which He hates' 12:31 and 16:22 alone, 'abomination of the LORD' eight in the book (7:25; 12:31; 17:1; 18:12; 22:5; 23:19; 25:16; 27:15) and eleven in Proverbs, 'abomination' seventeen in the book; THE CHILDREN BURNED — Jeremiah 7:31's six shared tokens, 2 Kings 17:31's four, Leviticus 18:21's three; THE KING-WORD'S HOMOGRAPH — Molech's consonants are 'to the king' (the census's slip at 7:8 and 11:3), 12:31 NAMES NO MOLECH (Leviticus 18:21 and 20:2-5 the kin, credited by name); THE DB'S 13:1 IS THE ENGLISH'S 12:32 ('you shall not add nor take away' — chapter 13's reading; the chapter thirty-one verses in the Hebrew numbering, computed from the verse table). Onkelos: 'destroys the nations' for cuts off at 12:29, 'their errors' for their gods at 12:30 and 12:31, 'before the LORD' for 'to the LORD' at 12:31. 		—Onkelos Deut 12:29, Onkelos Deut 12:30, Onkelos Deut 12:31, Sifrei Devarim 80:1, Sifrei Devarim 80:2, Sifrei Devarim 80:3, Sifrei Devarim 80:4, Sifrei Devarim 80:5, Sifrei Devarim 81:1, Sifrei Devarim 81:2, Sifrei Devarim 81:3, Sifrei Devarim 81:4, Sifrei Devarim 81:5, Sifrei Devarim 81:6, Sifrei Devarim 179:2witnessed
